## `AGO-1079-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Spectrometry Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Condiment Amino Acid Nitrogen & Metal HPLC Lab
- **Intro:** Spectrometry Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Condiment Amino Acid Nitrogen & Metal HPLC Lab — Pre-startup System Verification: Baseline drift ≤ 0.002 AU over 5 minutes; pump pressure 0 bar at start; solvent purity ≥ 99.9% HPLC grade; pH 2.50 ± 0.02.
- **Key tolerance:** Baseline drift ≤ 0.002 AU over 5 minutes; pump pressure 0 bar at start; solvent purity ≥ 99.9% HPLC grade; pH 2.50 ± 0.02.
- **Standards:** `ISO 17025:2017`, `Method 982.30`, `ISO 11885:2007`, `NA 123:2019`
